SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[ClientId], [t].[TotalPrice], [t].[status], [t0].[CartItemId], [t0].[CartId], [t0].[InventoryId], [t0].[ProductId], [t0].[Quantity], [t0].[SubTotal], [t0].[UnitPrice], [t0].[ProductId0], [t0].[ActiveIngredients], [t0].[AverageRating], [t0].[CategoryId], [t0].[CompanyId], [t0].[Contraindications], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[Description], [t0].[DiscountPercentage], [t0].[DosageForm], [t0].[IsAvailable], [t0].[IsDeleted], [t0].[MedicalDescription], [t0].[NameAr], [t0].[NameEn], [t0].[Price], [t0].[SideEffects], [t0].[Tags], [t0].[TotalRatings], [t0].[UpdatedAt], [t0].[Id], [t0].[IsPrimary], [t0].[ProductId1], [t0].[Url]

    FROM [Cart] AS [c]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = @__id_0
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [c0].[CartItemId], [c0].[CartId], [c0].[InventoryId], [c0].[ProductId], [c0].[Quantity], [c0].[SubTotal], [c0].[UnitPrice], [p].[ProductId] AS [ProductId0], [p].[ActiveIngredients], [p].[AverageRating], [p].[CategoryId], [p].[CompanyId], [p].[Contraindications], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[DiscountPercentage], [p].[DosageForm], [p].[IsAvailable], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[MedicalDescription], [p].[NameAr], [p].[NameEn], [p].[Price], [p].[SideEffects], [p].[Tags], [p].[TotalRatings], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[IsPrimary], [p0].[ProductId] AS [ProductId1], [p0].[Url]

    END AS [Discriminator], [u].[Id] AS [Id0], [u].[AdditionalInfo], [u].[AddressLine], [u].[ClientId] AS [ClientId0], [u].[IsDeleted] AS [IsDeleted0], [u].[IsPrimary], [u].[Label], [u].[Latitude], [u].[Longitude], [s].[Id] AS [Id1], [s].[Address], [s].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [s].[GeoLocation], [s].[IsDeleted] AS [IsDeleted1], [s].[Latitude] AS [Latitude0], [s].[Longitude] AS [Longitude0], [s].[Name], [s].[Phone], [p].[Id] AS [Id2], [p].[Amount], [p].[ClientPaymentToken], [p].[CompletedAt], [p].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt1], [p].[Method], [p].[OrderId], [p].[ProviderReferenceId], [p].[Status] AS [Status0], [p].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [p].[Version], [c].[Id] AS [Id3], [c].[AccountType], [c].[BirthDate], [c].[FavoritesCount], [c].[OrdersCount], [c].[RatesCount], [a].[Id] AS [Id4], [a].[AccessFailedCount], [a].[ConcurrencyStamp], [a].[Email], [a].[EmailConfirmed], [a].[FirstName], [a].[Gender], [a].[IsDeleted] AS [IsDeleted2], [a].[LastName], [a].[LockoutEnabled], [a].[LockoutEnd], [a].[NormalizedEmail], [a].[NormalizedUserName], [a].[OTP], [a].[OTPAttempts], [a].[OTPExpiryTime], [a].[PasswordHash], [a].[PhoneNumber], [a].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [a].[ProfileImageUrl], [a].[RefreshToken], [a].[RefreshTokenExpiryTime], [a].[ResetPasswordConfirmed], [a].[SecurityStamp], [a].[TwoFactorEnabled], [a].[UserName]
    FROM [Order] AS [o]
    LEFT JOIN [OnlineOrders] AS [o0] ON [o].[Id] = [o0].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [FromStoreOrders] AS [f] ON [o].[Id] = [f].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [UserAddress] AS [u] ON [o0].[ShippingAddressId] = [u].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [Store] AS [s] ON [f].[StoreId] = [s].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [Payment] AS [p] ON [o].[Id] = [p].[OrderId]
    LEFT JOIN [Clients] AS [c] ON [o].[ClientId] = [c].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [c].[Id] = [a].[Id]
    WHERE [o].[Id] = @__orderId_0
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [o1].[Id], [o1].[InvetoryId], [o1].[OrderId], [o1].[ProductId], [o1].[Quantity], [o1].[ReservationId], [o1].[SubTotal], [o1].[UnitPrice], [p0].[ProductId] AS [ProductId0], [p0].[ActiveIngredients], [p0].[AverageRating], [p0].[CategoryId], [p0].[CompanyId], [p0].[Contraindications], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[DiscountPercentage], [p0].[DosageForm], [p0].[IsAvailable], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[MedicalDescription], [p0].[NameAr], [p0].[NameEn], [p0].[Price], [p0].[SideEffects], [p0].[Tags], [p0].[TotalRatings], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[IsPrimary], [p1].[ProductId] AS [ProductId1], [p1].[Url]
    FROM [OrderItem] AS [o1]
    INNER JOIN [Products] AS [p0] ON [o1].[ProductId] = [p0].[ProductId]
    LEFT JOIN [ProductImage] AS [p1] ON [p0].[ProductId] = [p1].[ProductId]
) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[OrderId]
